## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner on this Mac. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 이 컴퓨터에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라거나, 특정 폴더의 문서를 정리해 달라고 하시면 바로 맡아서 진행할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先して届けることを保証しない通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認し、順序を揃え、再送もしてくれるので、Webやメールに向いています。UDPはそうした確認をしない分軽く速いので、動画配信やオンラインゲームに使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de compartirla, sobre todo si nadie más ha trabajado en ella. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir commits que otros tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的传输：先握手，保证数据不丢、不乱、按序到达；UDP 则是无连接、不保证可靠，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history: it replays your branch's commits on top of the latest base, which suits private or unshared feature branches before a PR.

Merge when the branch is already shared or its history matters: it keeps the real timeline and never rewrites commits others may have pulled.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the database is written down before it is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart, so committed changes survive and half-finished ones can be rolled back.
```

(function () {

      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
      if (t.price < THRESHOLD) val.style.color = 'var(--d-good)';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    if (under.length) {
      var min = Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function (t) { return t.price; }));
      document.getElementById('best').textContent = '最低 ¥' + min;
    } else {
      document.getElementById('best').textContent = '暂时没有 600 以下的';
    }
    document.getElementById('stamp').textContent = state && state.updated ? '更新于 ' + state.updated : '';
  }
  if (window.ducter) {
    render(window.ducter.get('state'));
    window.ducter.onChange(function () { render(window.ducter.get('state')); });
  }
})();

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，夜里的写入速度和延迟都会记在这个看板上，card 在下面。
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2186 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">No runs yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark, every night at 23:00</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>Nothing logged yet. The next run will show up here.</div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k' : String(n); }
function row(r){
  var el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'k-row';
  var main = document.createElement('div');
  main.className = 'k-row-main';
  main.textContent = r.night;
  var sub = document.createElement('div');
  sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
  sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · ' + r.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
  main.appendChild(sub);
  var val = document.createElement('div');
  val.className = 'k-row-value';
  val.textContent = fmt(r.writes) + ' writes/s';
  el.appendChild(main);
  el.appendChild(val);
  return el;
}
function render(runs){
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
  if (!runs.length) {
    empty.hidden = false;
    document.getElementById('latest').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">No runs yet</span>';
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var newest = runs[runs.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(newest.writes) + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = newest.night + ' · p99 ' + newest.p99 + ' ms · ' + newest.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){ list.appendChild(row(r)); });
}
render(ducter.get('runs'));
ducter.onChange(function(){ render(ducter.get('runs')); });
ducter.summary(function(){
  var runs = ducter.get('runs');
  if (!Array.isArray(runs) || !runs.length) return 'No runs yet';
  var n = runs[runs.length - 1];
  return n.night + ': ' + fmt(n.writes) + ' writes/s, p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms';
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

  <section class="k-card" style="margin-top:12px">
    <div class="k-field"><label>做了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="w" placeholder="例如 午饭、打车"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱</label><input class="k-num" id="a" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:8px">
      <button class="k-btn primary" id="btn" onclick="submitItem()" style="flex:1">记一笔</button>
      <button class="k-btn" id="del" onclick="removeItem()" style="display:none">删除</button>
      <button class="k-btn" id="cancel" onclick="resetForm()" style="display:none">取消</button>
    </div>
  </section>

  <div id="days"></div>
</main>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fmt = n => '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '');

let st = duct … l += '<h3 class="k-h">第' + d + '天 · ' + fmt(dayTotal) + '</h3>';
    html += rows.length ? '<div class="k-list k-card">' + rows.join('') + '</div>'
                        : '<div class="k-empty">这天还没记</div>';
  }
  $('days').innerHTML = html;
}

function pick(d) { cur = d; resetForm(); render(); }
function addDay() { st.days++; cur = st.days; save(); render(); }

function submitItem() {
  const w = $('w').value.trim();
  const a = parseFloat($('a').value);
  if (!w || !(a > 0)) return;
  if (edit >= 0) st.items[edit] = { d: st.items[edit].d, w: w, a: a };
  else st.items.push({ d: cur, w: w, a: a });
  save();
  resetForm();
  render();
}

function editItem(idx) {
  const it = st.items[idx];
  edit = idx;
  $('w').value = it.w;
  $('a').value = it.a;
  $('btn').textContent = '保存';
  $('del').style.display = '';
  $('cancel').style.display = '';
  $('w').focus();
}

function removeItem() {
  if (edit < 0) return;
  st.items.splice(edit, 1);
  save();
  resetForm();
  render();
}

function resetForm() {
  edit = -1;
  $('w').value = '';
  $('a').value = '';
  $('btn').textContent = '记一笔';
  $('del').style.display = 'none';
  $('cancel').style.display = 'none';
}

render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如:晚饭"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额(元)</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-24"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记上</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
function money(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  let sum=0;
  for(const x of xs) sum+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=money(sum);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=money(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(sum));
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan)) return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
